# Anti-Omission Enumeration (agent 2, Gate 5)

Report reviewed: RESEARCH-REPORT-review-panel-unification-run2.md (5,098 lines)
Date: 2026-11-14
Status: Complete

Method: one numbered list per assigned file; each list ends with `COUNT <file name>: <n>`. The two value ledgers (21 and 20) are enumerated row by row and compared by meaning (statement, kind, destination) with the inventory row of Appendix A (`synthesis/synth-08-value-inventory.md`, the file Section 8.1.2 and Appendix A name). A row id is used as a locator only; the statement and kind of each row were compared text-for-text and the destination was compared with the ledger destination or a recorded `moved from` / `DROP[duplicate]` reason.

## research/06-reflect-two-engines.md (distinct findings; R = REPRESENTED, location = report line; O = OMITTED, severity label in brackets; every O is "minor under grading override")
